Title: Gennadii Scholarii Patriarchae Constantinopolitani Defensio quinque capitum, quae in sancta oecumenica Florentina synodo continentur; Fabio Beneuolentio Senensi interprete
Creator: Georgios (Gennadios) Scholarios, later Patriarch Gennadios II; Latin translation by Fabio Benevento of Siena (first printed 1579, this is the 1637 Rome reprint)
Publisher: Rome: Typis Sacrae Congregationis de Propaganda Fide, 1637 ('ROMAE M. DC. XXXVII.' on the title page)
Publication Date: 1637
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: the title page reads 'ROMAE M. DC. XXXVII. Typis Sacrae Congreg. de Propag. Fide' (1637) in this scan's OCR (file line 37); the item record carries the Public Domain Mark 1.0 licence (creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/).
Language: lat (Latin translation of a Greek text; the Greek is not in the file)
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_Qww1OE7XzgMC (archive.org/details/bub_gb_Qww1OE7XzgMC), full-text file bub_gb_Qww1OE7XzgMC_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the Library thread, October 2026.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the OCR text follows it unchanged. Nothing was stripped, normalised, corrected, re-spaced or retyped (the long s glyph occurs 0 times and was left as found). The OCR text that follows is 464347 bytes, 461937 characters and 14017 lines. OCR quality sampled at the title pages and at about 5%, 50% and 95% of the file (lines 713, 7021 and 13329): Latin legible with long s read as f; ordinary slips.
Content note: A sixteenth-century Latin version of Scholarios's defence of the five chapters decided at the Council of Florence. Scholarios later became the first patriarch under the Ottomans; the file does not date the Greek original. The translator's dedication to Gregory XIII (the book was first printed in 1579) comes first. The Greek original is not in this file. It is a Latin witness to Gennadios's pre-1453 voice. Two other scans of the 1579 printing exist (bub_gb_yvYyx-fEwFkC, bub_gb_YKv6gjltRXMC, ita-bnc-mag-00002640-001); this 1637 reprint was chosen for the cleaner OCR.
